Pad to same length\n        max_len = max(len(d) for d in data_3c)\n        for i in range(len(data_3c)):\n            if len(data_3c[i]) < max_len:\n                data_3c[i] = np.pad(data_3c[i], (0, max_len - len(data_3c[i])), mode='constant')\n\n        # Prepare 3-component array\n        if z_data is not None and e_data is not None and n_data is not None:\n            max_len = max(len(z_data), len(e_data), len(n_data))\n\n            # Pad to same length\n            z_pad = np.pad(z_data, (0, max_len - len(z_data)), mode='constant')\n            e_pad = np.pad(e_data, (0, max_len - len(e_data)), mode='constant')\n            n_pad = np.pad(n_data, (0, max_len - len(n_data)), mode='constant')\n\n            # Stack as (samples, 3): order is typically E, N, Z for seisbench\n            data_stacked = np.stack([e_pad, n_pad, z_pad], axis=1).astype(np.float32)\n\n            # Process in windows\n            window_size = 3000  # 30 seconds at 100 Hz\n            stride = 1500  # 50% overlap\n\n            for start_idx in range(0, len(data_stacked) - window_size + 1, stride):\n                window = data_stacked[start_idx:start_idx + window_size]\n\n                # Sort traces by channel to get consistent ordering\n        traces_group = sorted(traces_group, key=lambda t: t.stats.channel)\n\n        # Get the actual UTCDateTime from the first trace\n        time_key = traces_group[0].stats.starttime\n\n        # Extract 3-component data\n        data_3c = []\n        sampling_rate = None\n\n        for trace in traces_group:\n            if sampling_rate is None:\n                sampling_rate = trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # Normalize each trace\n            data = trace.data.astype(np.float32)\n            data_std = np.std(data)\n            if data_std > 0:\n                data = data / data_std\n            else:\n                data = np.zeros_like(data)\n\n            data_3c.append(data)\n\n        # Pad to same length\n        max_len = max(len(d) for d in data_3c)\n        for i in range(len(data_3c)):\n            if len(data_3c[i]) < max_len:\n                data_3c[i] = np.pad(data_3c[i], (0, max_len - len(data_3c[i])), mode='constant')\n\n        # Stack into (samples, 3) for 3-component\n        if len(data_3c) >= 3:\n            data_stacked = np.stack(data_3c[:3], axis=1).astype(np.float32)\n        elif len(data_3c) > 0:\n            # If less than 3 components, duplicate the available ones\n            data_stacked = np.stack(data_3c * (3 // len(data_3c) + 1), axis=1)[:, :3].astype(np.float32)\n        else:\n            continue\n\n        # Reshape to (1, samples, 3) for batch processing\n            window_batch = window.reshape(1, window.shape[0], 3)\n\n            try:\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    predictions = model.predict(window_batch)\n\n                if predictions is not None:\n                    pred = predictions[0]  # Group by station and phase, keep best confidence\n    picks_df = picks_df.sort_values('confidence', ascending=False)\n    picks_df = picks_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station', 'phase'], keep='first')\n    picks_df = picks_df.sort_values('time')\n\n    print(f\"After removing duplicates: {len(picks_df)} unique picks\")\nelse:\n    print(\"Warning: No picks found\")\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(columns=['time'])\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    exit(0)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 2: Phase picking completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 3: Associate picks to identify events\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 3: Associating phase picks to identify earthquake events...\")\n\n# Use velocity model\nvp = 6.0  # km/s\nvs = vp / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Group picks by time window\nevents = []\nused_picks = set()\n\nfor idx, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n    if idx in used_picks:\n        continue\n\n    current_time = pick['time']\n    current_station = pick['station']\n    current_network = pick['network']\n\n    # Find all picks within time window (P-S time window ~ 30 seconds for nearby events)\n    time_window = 30  # seconds\n\n    event_picks = []\n    station_picks_dict = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for idx2, pick2 in picks_df.iterrows():\n        if idx2 in used_picks:\n            continue\n\n        # Time difference in seconds\n        time_diff = (pick2['time'] - current_time).total_seconds() if hasattr(pick2['time'] - current_time, 'total_seconds') else abs(float(pick2['time']) - float(current_time))\n\n        if abs(time_diff) <= time_window:\n            event_picks.append(idx2)\n            used_picks.add(idx2)\n            station_key = f\"{pick2['network']}.{pick2['station']}\"\n            station_picks_dict[station_key].append({\n                'phase': pick2['phase'],\n                'time': pick2['time'],\n                'channel': pick2['channel']\n            })\n\n    # Only consider as event if we have picks from multiple stations\n    unique_stations = len(station_picks_dict)\n\n    if unique_stations >= 2:  # Need at least 2 stations\n        # Get representative time (earliest P-wave)\n        event_pick_rows = picks_df.loc[event_picks]\n        p_picks = event_pick_rows[event_pick_rows['phase'] == 'P']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks.iloc[0]['time']\n        else:\n            event_time = event_pick_rows.iloc[0]['time']\n\n        # Check if we have reasonable P-S timing\n        has_ps_pair = False\n        for station_key, station_picks in station_picks_dict.items():\n            phases = [p['phase'] for p in station_picks]\n            if 'P' in phases and 'S' in phases:\n                has_ps_pair = True\n                break\n\n        # Add event if it has P-S pairs or has enough stations\n        if has_ps_pair or unique_stations >= 3:\n            events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': unique_stations,\n                'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n                'has_ps': has_ps_pair\n            })\n\nprint(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 3: Phase association completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: Write results to CSV\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 4: Writing results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Convert time to ISO format without timezone\n    results_df['time'] = results_df['time'].apply(\n        lambda x: x.isoformat() if hasattr(x, 'isoformat') else str(x)\n    )\n\n    # Keep only required columns for output\n    output_df = results_df[['time']]\n\n    output_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results_df)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n    print(\"\\nFirst few events:\")\n    print(output_df.head(10))\nelse:\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/seisbench-model-api\n\n# SeisBench Model API\n\n## Installing SeisBench\nThe recommended way is installation through pip. Simply run:\n```\npip install seisbench\n```\n\n## Overview\n\nSeisBench offers the abstract class `WaveformModel` that every SeisBench model should subclass. This class offers two core functions, `annotate` and `classify`. Both of the functions are automatically generated based on configurations and submethods implemented in the specific model.\n\nThe `SeisBenchModel` bridges the gap between the pytorch interface of the models and the obspy interface common in seismology. It automatically assembles obspy streams into pytorch tensors and reassembles the results into streams. It also takes care of batch processing. Computations can be run on GPU by simply moving the model to GPU.\n\nThe `annotate` function takes an obspy stream object as input and returns annotations as stream again. For example, for picking models the output would be the characteristic functions, i.e., the pick probabilities over time.\n\n```python\nstream = obspy.read(\"my_waveforms.mseed\")\nannotations = model.annotate(stream)  # Returns obspy stream object with annotations\n```\n\nThe `classify` function also takes an obspy stream as input, but in contrast to the `annotate` function returns discrete results. The structure of these results might be model dependent. For example, a pure picking model will return a list of picks, while a picking and detection model might return a list of picks and a list of detections.\n\n```python\nstream = obspy.read(\"my_waveforms.mseed\")\noutputs = model.classify(stream)  # Returns a list of picks\nprint(outputs)\n```\n\nBoth `annotate` and `classify` can be supplied with waveforms from multiple stations at once and will automatically handle the correct grouping of the traces. For details on how to build your own model with SeisBench, check the documentation of `WaveformModel`. For details on how to apply models, check out the Examples.\n\n## Loading Pretrained Models\n\nFor annotating waveforms in a meaningful way, trained model weights are required. SeisBench offers a range of pretrained model weights through a common interface. Model weights are downloaded on the first use and cached locally afterwards. For some model weights, multiple versions are available. For details on accessing these, check the documentation at `from_pretrained`.\n\n```python\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\nsbm.PhaseNet.list_pretrained()                  # Get available models\nmodel = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")  # Load the original model weights released by PhaseNet authors\n```\n\nPretrained models can not only be used for annotating data, but also offer a great starting point for transfer learning.\n\n## Speeding Up Model Application\n\nWhen applying models to large datasets, run time is often a major concern. Here are a few tips to make your model run faster:\n\n- **Run on GPU.** Execution on GPU is usually faster, even though exact speed-ups vary between models. However, we note that running on GPU is not necessarily the most economic option. For example, in cloud applications it might be cheaper (and equally fast) to pay for a handful of CPU machines to annotate a large dataset than for a GPU machine.\n\n- **Use a large `batch_size`.** This parameter can be passed as an optional argument to all models. Especially on GPUs, larger batch sizes lead to faster annotations. As long as the batch fits into (GPU) memory, it might be worth increasing the batch size.\n\n- **Compile your model (torch 2.0+).** If you are using torch in version 2.0 or newer, compile your model. It's as simple as running `model = torch.compile(model)`. The compilation will take some time but if you are annotating large amounts of waveforms, it should pay off quickly. Note that there are many options for compile that might influence the performance gains considerably.\n\n- **Use asyncio interface.** Load data in parallel while executing the model using the asyncio interface, i.e., `annotate_asyncio` and `classify_asyncio`. This is usually substantially faster because data loading is IO-bound while the actual annotation is compute-bound.\n\n- **Manual resampling.** While SeisBench can automatically resample the waveforms, it can be faster to do the resampling manually beforehand. SeisBench uses obspy routines for resampling, which (as of 2023) are not parallelised. Check the required sampling rate with `model.sampling_rate`. Alternative routines are available, e.g., in the Pyrocko library.\n\n## Models Integrated into SeisBench\n\nYou don't have to build models from scratch if you don't want to. SeisBench integrates the following notable models from the literature for you to use. Again, as they inherit from the common SeisBench model interface, all these deep learning models are constructed through PyTorch. Where possible, the original trained weights are imported and made available. These can be accessed via the `from_pretrained` method.\n\n| Integrated Model | Task |\n|------------------|------|\n| `BasicPhaseAE` | Phase Picking |\n| `CRED` | Earthquake Detection |\n| `DPP` | Phase Picking |\n| `DepthPhaseNet` | Depth estimation from depth phases |\n| `DepthPhaseTEAM` | Depth estimation from depth phases |\n| `DeepDenoiser` | Denoising |\n| `SeisDAE` | Denoising |\n| `EQTransformer` | Earthquake Detection/Phase Picking |\n| `GPD` | Phase Picking |\n| `LFEDetect` | Phase Picking (Low-frequency earthquakes) |\n| `OBSTransformer` | Earthquake Detection/Phase Picking |\n| `PhaseNet` | Phase Picking |\n| `PhaseNetLight` | Phase Picking |\n| `PickBlue` | Earthquake Detection/Phase Picking |\n| `Skynet` | Phase Picking |\n| `VariableLengthPhaseNet` | Phase Picking |\n\nCurrently integrated models are capable of earthquake detection and phase picking, waveform denoising, depth estimation, and low-frequency earthquake phase picking. Furthermore, with SeisBench you can build ML models to perform general seismic tasks such as magnitude and source parameter estimation, hypocentre determination etc.\n\n## Best Practices\n- If the waveform data happen to be extremely small in scale (`<=1e-10`), there might be risk of numerical instability. It is acceptable to increase the value first (by multiplying a large number like `1e10`) before normalization or passing to the model.\n\n- Although the seisbench model API will normalize the waveform for you, it is still highly suggested to apply normalization yourself. Since seisbench's normalization scheme uses an epsilon `(waveform - mean(waveform)) / (std(waveform) + epsilon)`, for extremely small values (such as `<=1e-10`), their normalization can destroy the signals in the waveform.\n\n- The seisbench model API can process a stream of waveform data of arbitrary length. Hence, it is not necessary to segment the data yourself. In addition, you should not assume a stream of waveform can only contain one P-wave and one S-wave. It is the best to treat the stream like what it is: a stream of continuous data.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5ffeff1c-4550-4869-8f27-42db1c9ef41b","uuid":"ca906395-2623-4aef-8c56-46256584cf23","isSynthetic":true}

Load waveform data (MSEED) and station information\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench deep learning models\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read, Stream\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\nfrom seisbench.models import EQTransformer\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nprint(\"Loading waveform and station data...\")\n\n# Load waveform data\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces from MSEED file\")\n\n# Load station data\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 1: Loading data completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 2: Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 2: Picking P and S waves with deep learning models...\")\n\n# Load pre-trained EQTransformer model\nmodel = EQTransformer.from_pretrained(\"original\")\nprint(\"Loaded EQTransformer model\")\n\n# Group traces by station and process each group\ntraces_by_station_time = defaultdict(list)\n\nfor trace in st:\n    station_key = f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}\"\n    time_key = str(trace.stats.starttime)\n    key = (station_key, time_key)\n    traces_by_station_time[key].append(trace)\n\nprint(f\"Organized {len(st)} traces into {len(traces_by_station_time)} station-time groups\")\n\npicks_list = []\n\n# Process each station group\nfor group_idx, ((station_key, time_key_str), traces_group) in enumerate(traces_by_station_time.items()):\n    if group_idx % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processing {group_idx}/{len(traces_by_station_time)} groups ({len(picks_list)} picks found)...\")\n\n    try:\n        # Create a proper obspy Stream for this station\n        stream = Stream(traces_group)\n\n        # Apply the model's classify method to get picks\n        # This returns picks directly from the model\n        picks = model.classify(stream, overlap=100, batch_size=12)\n\n        if picks is not None and len(picks) > 0:\n            for pick in picks:\n                picks_list.append({\n                    'network': pick.network,\n                    'station': pick.station,\n                    'location': pick.location if hasattr(pick, 'location') else '',\n                    'channel': pick.channel if hasattr(pick, 'channel') else '',\n                    'phase': pick.phase,\n                    'time': pick.time,\n                    'confidence': float(pick.confidence) if hasattr(pick, 'confidence') else 0.5\n                })\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks found: {len(picks_list)}\")\n\nif len(picks_list) == 0:\n    print(\"Warning: No picks found\")\n    # Create empty results\n    pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    exit(0)\n\n# Convert to DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n# Remove duplicate picks from same station (keep highest confidence)\npicks_df = picks_df.sort_values('confidence', ascending=False)\npicks_df = picks_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station', 'phase'], keep='first')\npicks_df = picks_df.sort_values('time')\n\nprint(f\"After deduplication: {len(picks_df)} unique picks\")\nprint(f\"\\nPhase pick distribution:\")\nprint(picks_df.groupby(['phase', 'station']).size())\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 2: Phase picking completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 3: Associate picks to identify events\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 3: Associating phase picks to identify earthquake events...\")\n\n# Convert time to timestamp for easier comparison\npicks_df['timestamp'] = picks_df['time'].apply(lambda x: x.timestamp)\n\nevents = []\nused_picks = set()\n\n# Use clustering to group picks by time\nfor idx, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n    if idx in used_picks:\n        continue\n\n    current_time = pick['timestamp']\n\n    # Time window for event clustering\n    time_window = 20  # 20 seconds\n\n    # Find all picks within time window\n    event_pick_indices = []\n    station_dict_event = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for idx2, pick2 in picks_df.iterrows():\n        if idx2 in used_picks:\n            continue\n\n        time_diff = abs(pick2['timestamp'] - current_time)\n\n        if time_diff <= time_window:\n            event_pick_indices.append(idx2)\n            used_picks.add(idx2)\n\n            sta_key = f\"{pick2['network']}.{pick2['station']}\"\n            station_dict_event[sta_key].append({\n                'phase': pick2['phase'],\n                'time': pick2['time'],\n                'timestamp': pick2['timestamp']\n            })\n\n    # Require minimum stations or P-S pairs\n    num_stations = len(station_dict_event)\n\n    # Check for P-S pairs\n    has_ps_pair = False\n    for sta, sta_picks in station_dict_event.items():\n        phases = [p['phase'] for p in sta_picks]\n        if 'P' in phases and 'S' in phases:\n            has_ps_pair = True\n            break\n\n    # Create event if criteria met\n    if num_stations >= 3 or (num_stations >= 2 and has_ps_pair):\n        # Use earliest P-wave as event time\n        event_pick_rows = picks_df.loc[event_pick_indices]\n        p_picks = event_pick_rows[event_pick_rows['phase'] == 'P']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks.iloc[0]['time']\n        else:\n            event_time = event_pick_rows.iloc[0]['time']\n\n        events.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_stations': num_stations,\n            'num_picks': len(event_pick_indices),\n            'has_ps': has_ps_pair\n        })\n\nprint(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    print(\"\\nEvent summary:\")\n    for i, evt in enumerate(events[:10]):\n        print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {evt['time'].isoformat()} ({evt['num_stations']} stations, {evt['num_picks']} picks)\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 3: Phase association completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: Write results to CSV\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 4: Writing results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Convert to ISO format\n    results_df['time'] = results_df['time'].apply(\n        lambda x: x.isoformat() if hasattr(x, 'isoformat') else str(x)\n    )\n\n    # Write only time column\n    output_df = results_df[['time']]\n    output_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results_df)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n    print(\"\\nFirst 10 events:\")\n    print(output_df.head(10).to_string())\nelse:\n    print(\"No events identified\")\n    pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Workflow completed ===\")\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1843,"cache_read_input_tokens":50213,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1843,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5ffeff1c-4550-4869-8f27-42db1c9ef41b","uuid":"54a5bad1-c607-4ff0-8f2d-1d7a8897ab0d"}

python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic phase association workflow:\n1. Load waveform data (MSEED) and station information\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench deep learning models\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read, Stream\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\nfrom seisbench.models import EQTransformer\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nprint(\"Loading waveform and station data...\")\n\n# Load waveform data\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces from MSEED file\")\n\n# Load station data\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 1: Loading data completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 2: Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 2: Picking P and S waves with deep learning models...\")\n\n# Load pre-trained EQTransformer model\nmodel = EQTransformer.from_pretrained(\"original\")\nprint(\"Loaded EQTransformer model\")\n\n# Group traces by station and process each group\ntraces_by_station_time = defaultdict(list)\n\nfor trace in st:\n    station_key = f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}\"\n    time_key = str(trace.stats.starttime)\n    key = (station_key, time_key)\n    traces_by_station_time[key].append(trace)\n\nprint(f\"Organized {len(st)} traces into {len(traces_by_station_time)} station-time groups\")\n\npicks_list = []\n\n# Process each station group\nfor group_idx, ((station_key, time_key_str), traces_group) in enumerate(traces_by_station_time.items()):\n    if group_idx % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processing {group_idx}/{len(traces_by_station_time)} groups ({len(picks_list)} picks found)...\")\n\n    try:\n        # Create a proper obspy Stream for this station\n        stream = Stream(traces_group)\n\n        # Apply the model's classify method to get picks\n        # This returns picks directly from the model\n        picks = model.classify(stream, overlap=100, batch_size=12)\n\n        if picks is not None and len(picks) > 0:\n            for pick in picks:\n                picks_list.append({\n                    'network': pick.network,\n                    'station': pick.station,\n                    'location': pick.location if hasattr(pick, 'location') else '',\n                    'channel': pick.channel if hasattr(pick, 'channel') else '',\n                    'phase': pick.phase,\n                    'time': pick.time,\n                    'confidence': float(pick.confidence) if hasattr(pick, 'confidence') else 0.5\n                })\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks found: {len(picks_list)}\")\n\nif len(picks_list) == 0:\n    print(\"Warning: No picks found\")\n    # Time window for event clustering\n    time_window = 20  # 20 seconds\n\n    # Find all picks within time window\n    event_pick_indices = []\n    station_dict_event = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for idx2, pick2 in picks_df.iterrows():\n        if idx2 in used_picks:\n            continue\n\n        time_diff = abs(pick2['timestamp'] - current_time)\n\n        if time_diff <= time_window:\n            event_pick_indices.append(idx2)\n            used_picks.add(idx2)\n\n            sta_key = f\"{pick2['network']}.{pick2['station']}\"\n            station_dict_event[sta_key].append({\n                'phase': pick2['phase'],\n                'time': pick2['time'],\n                'timestamp': pick2['timestamp']\n            })\n\n    # Require minimum stations or P-S pairs\n    num_stations = len(station_dict_event)\n\n    # Check for P-S pairs\n    has_ps_pair = False\n    for sta, sta_picks in station_dict_event.items():\n        phases = [p['phase'] for p in sta_picks]\n        if 'P' in phases and 'S' in phases:\n            has_ps_pair = True\n            break\n\n    # Create event if criteria met\n    if num_stations >= 3 or (num_stations >= 2 and has_ps_pair):\n        # Use earliest P-wave as event time\n        event_pick_rows = picks_df.loc[event_pick_indices]\n        p_picks = event_pick_rows[event_pick_rows['phase'] == 'P']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks.iloc[0]['time']\n        else:\n            event_time = event_pick_rows.iloc[0]['time']\n\n        events.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_stations': num_stations,\n            'num_picks': len(event_pick_indices),\n            'has_ps': has_ps_pair\n        })\n\nprint(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    print(\"\\nEvent summary:\")\n    for i, evt in enumerate(events[:10]):\n        print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {evt['time'].isoformat()} ({evt['num_stations']} stations, {evt['num_picks']} picks)\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 3: Phase association completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: Write results to CSV\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 4: Writing results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Load waveform data (MSEED) and station information\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench deep learning models\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\nfrom seisbench.models import EQTransformer\nfrom seisbench.data import WaveformDataset, BenchmarkDataset\nimport torch\n\nprint(\"Loading waveform and station data...\")\n\n# Load waveform data\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces from MSEED file\")\n\n# Load station data\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 1: Loading data completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 2: Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 2: Picking P and S waves with deep learning models...\")\n\n# Load pre-trained EQTransformer model\nmodel = EQTransformer.from_pretrained(\"original\")\nprint(\"Loaded EQTransformer model\")\n\npicks_list = []\n\n# Group traces by station and starttime\nfrom collections import defaultdict\ntraces_by_station_time = defaultdict(list)\n\nfor trace in st:\n    station_key = f\"{trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}\"\n    time_key = str(trace.stats.starttime)\n    key = (station_key, time_key)\n    traces_by_station_time[key].append(trace)\n\nprint(f\"Organized {len(st)} traces into {len(traces_by_station_time)} station-time groups\")\n\n# Process each station group\nfor group_idx, ((station_key, time_key_str), traces_group) in enumerate(traces_by_station_time.items()):\n    if group_idx % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processing {group_idx}/{len(traces_by_station_time)} groups ({len(picks_list)} picks found)...\")\n\n    try:\n        # Extract 3-component data: HH* or BH* or HN* channels\n        data_3c = {}\n        for trace in traces_group:\n            ch = trace.stats.channel\n            # Normalize data to float32\n            data = trace.data.astype(np.float32)\n            # Normalize by std\n            std = np.std(data)\n            if std > 0:\n                data = data / std\n            data_3c[ch] = data\n\n        if len(data_3c) < 2:\n            continue\n\n        # Get components (E, N, Z)\n        channels = sorted(data_3c.keys())\n\n        # Try to find E, N, Z components\n        z_data = None\n        e_data = None\n        n_data = None\n\n        for ch in channels:\n            if ch.endswith('Z'):\n                z_data = data_3c[ch]\n            elif ch.endswith('E'):\n                e_data = data_3c[ch]\n            elif ch.endswith('N'):\n                n_data = data_3c[ch]\n\n        # Prepare 3-component array\n        if z_data is not None and e_data is not None and n_data is not None:\n            max_len = max(len(z_data), len(e_data), len(n_data))\n\n            # Pad to same length\n            z_pad = np.pad(z_data, (0, max_len - len(z_data)), mode='constant')\n            e_pad = np.pad(e_data, (0, max_len - len(e_data)), mode='constant')\n            n_pad = np.pad(n_data, (0, max_len - len(n_data)), mode='constant')\n\n            # Stack as (samples, 3): order is typically E, N, Z for seisbench\n            data_stacked = np.stack([e_pad, n_pad, z_pad], axis=1).astype(np.float32)\n\n            # Process in windows\n            window_size = 3000  # 30 seconds at 100 Hz\n            stride = 1500  # 50% overlap\n\n            for start_idx in range(0, len(data_stacked) - window_size + 1, stride):\n                window = data_stacked[start_idx:start_idx + window_size]\n\n                # Reshape for batch: (batch_size, samples, 3)\n                batch = window.reshape(1, window.shape[0], 3)\n\n                try:\n                    # Get predictions\n                    with torch.no_grad():\n                        pred = model.predict(batch)\n\n                    if pred is not None and len(pred) > 0:\n                        pred_data = pred[0]  # Get first batch\n\n                        # pred_data shape: (samples, 4) for [P, S, noise, ...]\n                        if len(pred_data.shape) == 2 and pred_data.shape[1] >= 2:\n                            p_pred = pred_data[:, 0]  # P probability\n                            s_pred = pred_data[:, 1]  # S probability\n\n                            # Find strong predictions\n                            p_threshold = 0.4\n                            s_threshold = 0.4\n\n                            # Find picks (use argmax for robustness)\n                            p_picks_idx = np.where(p_pred > p_threshold)[0]\n                            s_picks_idx = np.where(s_pred > s_threshold)[0]\n\n                            # Get first occurrence\n                            if len(p_picks_idx) > 0:\n                                p_sample_idx = int(p_picks_idx[0])\n                                absolute_idx = start_idx + p_sample_idx\n\n                                if absolute_idx < 360000:  # Ensure within bounds\n                                    p_time = ref_time + absolute_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    picks_list.append({\n                                        'network': traces_group[0].stats.network,\n                                        'station': traces_group[0].stats.station,\n                                        'location': traces_group[0].stats.location,\n                                        'channel': 'Z',  # Use Z phase\n                                        'phase': 'P',\n                                        'time': p_time,\n                                        'confidence': float(p_pred[p_sample_idx])\n                                    })\n\n                            if len(s_picks_idx) > 0:\n                                s_sample_idx = int(s_picks_idx[0])\n                                absolute_idx = start_idx + s_sample_idx\n\n                                if absolute_idx < 360000:\n                                    s_time = ref_time + absolute_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    picks_list.append({\n                                        'network': traces_group[0].stats.network,\n                                        'station': traces_group[0].stats.station,\n                                        'location': traces_group[0].stats.location,\n                                        'channel': 'Z',\n                                        'phase': 'S',\n                                        'time': s_time,\n                                        'confidence': float(s_pred[s_sample_idx])\n                                    })\n\n                except Exception as e:\n                    continue\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks found: {len(picks_list)}\")\n\nif len(picks_list) == 0:\n    print(\"Warning: No picks found with default threshold\")\n    # Create empty results\n    pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    exit(0)\n\n# Convert to DataFrame and deduplicate\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\n\n# Remove duplicate picks from same station (keep highest confidence)\npicks_df = picks_df.sort_values('confidence', ascending=False)\npicks_df = picks_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station', 'phase'], keep='first')\npicks_df = picks_df.sort_values('time')\n\nprint(f\"After deduplication: {len(picks_df)} unique picks\")\nprint(f\"\\nPhase pick distribution:\")\nprint(picks_df.groupby(['phase', 'station']).size())\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 2: Phase picking completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 3: Associate picks to identify events\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 3: Associating phase picks to identify earthquake events...\")\n\n# Convert time to timestamp for easier comparison\npicks_df['timestamp'] = picks_df['time'].apply(lambda x: x.timestamp)\n\nevents = []\nused_picks = set()\n\n# Use a more sophisticated clustering approach\n# Group picks that are close in time and have multiple stations\nfor idx, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n    if idx in used_picks:\n        continue\n\n    current_time = pick['timestamp']\n\n    # Time window for event clustering\n    time_window = 20  # 20 seconds - allows for nearby events\n\n    # Find all picks within time window\n    event_pick_indices = []\n    station_dict_event = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for idx2, pick2 in picks_df.iterrows():\n        if idx2 in used_picks:\n            continue\n\n        time_diff = abs(pick2['timestamp'] - current_time)\n\n        if time_diff <= time_window:\n            event_pick_indices.append(idx2)\n            used_picks.add(idx2)\n\n            sta_key = f\"{pick2['network']}.{pick2['station']}\"\n            station_dict_event[sta_key].append({\n                'phase': pick2['phase'],\n                'time': pick2['time'],\n                'timestamp': pick2['timestamp']\n            })\n\n    # Require minimum 3 stations or 2 stations with P-S pair\n    num_stations = len(station_dict_event)\n\n    # Check for P-S pairs\n    has_ps_pair = False\n    for sta, picks in station_dict_event.items():\n        phases = [p['phase'] for p in picks]\n        if 'P' in phases and 'S' in phases:\n            has_ps_pair = True\n            break\n\n    # Create event if criteria met\n    if num_stations >= 3 or (num_stations >= 2 and has_ps_pair):\n        # Use earliest P-wave as event time\n        event_pick_rows = picks_df.loc[event_pick_indices]\n        p_picks = event_pick_rows[event_pick_rows['phase'] == 'P']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks.iloc[0]['time']\n        else:\n            event_time = event_pick_rows.iloc[0]['time']\n\n        events.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_stations': num_stations,\n            'num_picks': len(event_pick_indices),\n            'has_ps': has_ps_pair\n        })\n\nprint(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    print(\"\\nEvent summary:\")\n    for i, evt in enumerate(events[:5]):\n        print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {evt['time'].isoformat()} ({evt['num_stations']} stations, {evt['num_picks']} picks)\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 3: Phase association completed ===\\n\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: Write results to CSV\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"Step 4: Writing results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\nif len(events) > 0:\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Convert to ISO format\n    results_df['time'] = results_df['time'].apply(\n        lambda x: x.isoformat() if hasattr(x, 'isoformat') else str(x)\n    )\n\n    # Write only time column\n    output_df = results_df[['time']]\n    output_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results_df)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n    print(\"\\nFirst 10 events:\")\n    print(output_df.head(10).to_string())\nelse:\n    print(\"No events identified\")\n    pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Workflow completed ===\")\n"}}
